 }
 
+#ifdef __CUDACC__
+
+// The numerical kernels use one independent CUDA thread per matrix element (or
+// receiver).  The timestep loop remains on the host because each timestep
+// depends on the preceding one; each launch is a global device barrier.
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+
+namespace {
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dAdd(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dSub(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dMul(Vec3 a, val_t s) {
+    return Vec3(a.x * s, a.y * s, a.z * s);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dDot(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dCross(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.y * b.z - a.z * b.y, a.z * b.x - a.x * b.z,
+                a.x * b.y - a.y * b.x);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dNorm(Vec3 v) {
+    return sqrtf(dDot(v, v));
+}
+
+__device__ val_t dRayTriangle(Vec3 orig, Vec3 dir, const Triangle& tri) {
+    Vec3 e1 = dSub(tri.b, tri.a), e2 = dSub(tri.c, tri.a);
+    Vec3 p = dCross(dir, e2);
+    val_t det = dDot(e1, p);
+    if (fabsf(det) < EPSILON) return 1.0e30f;
+    val_t inv = 1.0f / det;
+    Vec3 t = dSub(orig, tri.a);
+    val_t u = dDot(t, p) * inv;
+    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return 1.0e30f;
+    Vec3 q = dCross(t, e1);
+    val_t v = dDot(dir, q) * inv;
+    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return 1.0e30f;
+    return dDot(e2, q) * inv;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t dHash(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU;
+    x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bU;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dRandom(uint32_t seed) {
+    return (dHash(seed) >> 8) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
+}
+__device__ Vec3 dSample(const Triangle& tri, uint32_t seed) {
+    val_t u = dRandom(seed), v = dRandom(seed + 0x9e3779b9U);
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return dAdd(tri.a, dAdd(dMul(dSub(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                           dMul(dSub(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* tau, size_t n) {
+    size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t count = n * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    size_t i = pair / n, j = pair % n;
+    if (i == j) { tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    Vec3 delta = dSub(dMul(dAdd(dAdd(triangles[i].a, triangles[i].b),
+                               triangles[i].c), 1.0f / 3.0f),
+                      dMul(dAdd(dAdd(triangles[j].a, triangles[j].b),
+                               triangles[j].c), 1.0f / 3.0f));
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(dNorm(delta) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void formFactorKernel(const Triangle* triangles, val_t* kij, size_t n) {
+    size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t count = n * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    size_t i = pair / n, j = pair % n;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    if (dDot(a._normal, b._normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        Vec3 p = dSample(a, static_cast<uint32_t>(pair * NUM_RAYS + r * 2 + 1));
+        Vec3 q = dSample(b, static_cast<uint32_t>(pair * NUM_RAYS + r * 2 + 2));
+        Vec3 v = dSub(q, p);
+        val_t length = dNorm(v);
+        if (length <= EPSILON) continue;
+        Vec3 dir = dMul(v, 1.0f / length);
+        bool blocked = false;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+            if (k == i || k == j) continue;
+            val_t hit = dRayTriangle(p, dir, triangles[k]);
+            if (hit > EPSILON && hit < length - EPSILON) { blocked = true; break; }
+        }
+        if (blocked) continue;
+        val_t d2 = dDot(v, v);
+        val_t ci = fmaxf(0.0f, dDot(v, a._normal) / length);
+        val_t cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -dDot(v, b._normal) / length);
+        if (ci > 0.0f && cj > 0.0f) sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  const val_t* emission, val_t* reflected,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (t < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[i * n + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t source = reflected[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+    }
+    reflected[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* reflected, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += reflected[ (tt - t) * n + source] * reflected[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+template <typename T> T* deviceCopy(const std::vector<T>& input) {
+    T* result = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&result), input.size() * sizeof(T)), "cudaMalloc");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(result, input.data(), input.size() * sizeof(T), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "cudaMemcpy H2D");
+    return result;
+}
+
+void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing time delays (Tau) on CUDA...\n");
+    Triangle* dTriangles = deviceCopy(s.triangles);
+    int* dTau = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dTau), s.tau.size() * sizeof(int)), "cudaMalloc tau");
+    size_t count = s.numTriangles * s.numTriangles;
+    timeDelayKernel<<<(count + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dTriangles, dTau, s.numTriangles);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "timeDelayKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "timeDelayKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.tau.data(), dTau, s.tau.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy tau");
+    cudaFree(dTau); cudaFree(dTriangles);
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij) on CUDA...\n");
+    Triangle* dTriangles = deviceCopy(s.triangles);
+    val_t* dKij = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dKij), s.kij.size() * sizeof(val_t)), "cudaMalloc Kij");
+    size_t count = s.numTriangles * s.numTriangles;
+    formFactorKernel<<<(count + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dTriangles, dKij, s.numTriangles);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "formFactorKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "formFactorKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.kij.data(), dKij, s.kij.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy Kij");
+    cudaFree(dKij); cudaFree(dTriangles);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Running wave propagation simulation on CUDA...\n");
+    int* dTau = deviceCopy(s.tau); val_t* dKij = deviceCopy(s.kij);
+    val_t* dAreas = deviceCopy(s.areas); val_t* dRho = deviceCopy(s.rho);
+    val_t* dEmission = deviceCopy(s.radE); val_t* dReflected = deviceCopy(s.radB);
+    size_t blocks = (s.numTriangles + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < s.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        propagationKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dEmission, dReflected, s.numTriangles, t);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "propagationKernel");
+    }
+    cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "propagation sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.radB.data(), dReflected, s.radB.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy radiosity");
+    cudaFree(dTau); cudaFree(dKij); cudaFree(dAreas); cudaFree(dRho); cudaFree(dEmission); cudaFree(dReflected);
+}
+
+void computeDistances(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation on CUDA...\n");
+    val_t* dReflected = deviceCopy(s.radB); val_t* dDistances = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dDistances), s.distances.size() * sizeof(val_t)), "cudaMalloc distances");
+    size_t blocks = (s.numTriangles + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    distanceKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dReflected, dDistances, s.numTriangles, s.numTimesteps, s.sourceIndex);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "distanceKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "distanceKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.distances.data(), dDistances, s.distances.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy distances");
+    cudaFree(dReflected); cudaFree(dDistances);
+}
+
+} // namespace
+#else
+// This branch is retained only so the translation unit remains readable to
+// host-side tools; CMake always compiles roomsim.cpp as CUDA.
+void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& s) { computeTimeDelaysCPU(s); }
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& s) { computeFormFactorsCPU(s); }
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& s) { runSimulationCPU(s); }
+void computeDistances(SimulationState& s) { computeDistancesCPU(s); }
+#endif

+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }

+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dAdd(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dSub(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dMul(Vec3 a, val_t s) {
+    return Vec3(a.x * s, a.y * s, a.z * s);

+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dDot(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dCross(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.y * b.z - a.z * b.y, a.z * b.x - a.x * b.z,
+                a.x * b.y - a.y * b.x);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dNorm(Vec3 v) {
+    return sqrtf(dDot(v, v));
+}

+__device__ val_t dRayTriangle(Vec3 orig, Vec3 dir, const Triangle& tri) {
+    Vec3 e1 = dSub(tri.b, tri.a), e2 = dSub(tri.c, tri.a);
+    Vec3 p = dCross(dir, e2);
+    val_t det = dDot(e1, p);
+    if (fabsf(det) < EPSILON) return 1.0e30f;

+    val_t u = dDot(t, p) * inv;
+    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return 1.0e30f;

+    val_t v = dDot(dir, q) * inv;
+    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return 1.0e30f;
+    return dDot(e2, q) * inv;
+}

+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU;
+    x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bU;

+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dRandom(uint32_t seed) {

+__device__ Vec3 dSample(const Triangle& tri, uint32_t seed) {

+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* tau, size_t n) {
+    size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t count = n * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    size_t i = pair / n, j = pair % n;

+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(dNorm(delta) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));

+__global__ void formFactorKernel(const Triangle* triangles, val_t* kij, size_t n) {
+    size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t count = n * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    size_t i = pair / n, j = pair % n;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    if (dDot(a._normal, b._normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        Vec3 p = dSample(a, static_cast<uint32_t>(pair * NUM_RAYS + r * 2 + 1));
+        Vec3 q = dSample(b, static_cast<uint32_t>(pair * NUM_RAYS + r * 2 + 2));
+        Vec3 v = dSub(q, p);
+        val_t length = dNorm(v);
+        if (length <= EPSILON) continue;
+        Vec3 dir = dMul(v, 1.0f / length);
+        bool blocked = false;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+            if (k == i || k == j) continue;
+            val_t hit = dRayTriangle(p, dir, triangles[k]);
+            if (hit > EPSILON && hit < length - EPSILON) { blocked = true; break; }
+        }
+        if (blocked) continue;
+        val_t d2 = dDot(v, v);
+        val_t ci = fmaxf(0.0f, dDot(v, a._normal) / length);
+        val_t cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -dDot(v, b._normal) / length);
+        if (ci > 0.0f && cj > 0.0f) sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  const val_t* emission, val_t* reflected,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (t < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[i * n + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t source = reflected[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+    }
+    reflected[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* reflected, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += reflected[ (tt - t) * n + source] * reflected[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+template <typename T> T* deviceCopy(const std::vector<T>& input) {
+    T* result = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&result), input.size() * sizeof(T)), "cudaMalloc");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(result, input.data(), input.size() * sizeof(T), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "cudaMemcpy H2D");
+    return result;
+}
+
+void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing time delays (Tau) on CUDA...\n");
+    Triangle* dTriangles = deviceCopy(s.triangles);
+    int* dTau = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dTau), s.tau.size() * sizeof(int)), "cudaMalloc tau");
+    size_t count = s.numTriangles * s.numTriangles;
+    timeDelayKernel<<<(count + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dTriangles, dTau, s.numTriangles);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "timeDelayKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "timeDelayKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.tau.data(), dTau, s.tau.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy tau");
+    cudaFree(dTau); cudaFree(dTriangles);
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij) on CUDA...\n");
+    Triangle* dTriangles = deviceCopy(s.triangles);
+    val_t* dKij = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dKij), s.kij.size() * sizeof(val_t)), "cudaMalloc Kij");
+    size_t count = s.numTriangles * s.numTriangles;
+    formFactorKernel<<<(count + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dTriangles, dKij, s.numTriangles);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "formFactorKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "formFactorKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.kij.data(), dKij, s.kij.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy Kij");
+    cudaFree(dKij); cudaFree(dTriangles);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Running wave propagation simulation on CUDA...\n");
+    int* dTau = deviceCopy(s.tau); val_t* dKij = deviceCopy(s.kij);
+    val_t* dAreas = deviceCopy(s.areas); val_t* dRho = deviceCopy(s.rho);
+    val_t* dEmission = deviceCopy(s.radE); val_t* dReflected = deviceCopy(s.radB);
+    size_t blocks = (s.numTriangles + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < s.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        propagationKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dEmission, dReflected, s.numTriangles, t);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "propagationKernel");
+    }
+    cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "propagation sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.radB.data(), dReflected, s.radB.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy radiosity");
+    cudaFree(dTau); cudaFree(dKij); cudaFree(dAreas); cudaFree(dRho); cudaFree(dEmission); cudaFree(dReflected);
+}
+
+void computeDistances(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation on CUDA...\n");
+    val_t* dReflected = deviceCopy(s.radB); val_t* dDistances = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dDistances), s.distances.size() * sizeof(val_t)), "cudaMalloc distances");
+    size_t blocks = (s.numTriangles + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    distanceKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dReflected, dDistances, s.numTriangles, s.numTimesteps, s.sourceIndex);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "distanceKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "distanceKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.distances.data(), dDistances, s.distances.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy distances");
+    cudaFree(dReflected); cudaFree(dDistances);
+}
+
+} // namespace
+#else
+// This branch is retained only so the translation unit remains readable to
+// host-side tools; CMake always compiles roomsim.cpp as CUDA.
+void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& s) { computeTimeDelaysCPU(s); }
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& s) { computeFormFactorsCPU(s); }
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& s) { runSimulationCPU(s); }
+void computeDistances(SimulationState& s) { computeDistancesCPU(s); }
+#endif

+#ifdef __CUDACC__
+
+// The numerical kernels use one independent CUDA thread per matrix element (or
+// receiver).  The timestep loop remains on the host because each timestep
+// depends on the preceding one; each launch is a global device barrier.
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+
+namespace {
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dAdd(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dSub(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dMul(Vec3 a, val_t s) {
+    return Vec3(a.x * s, a.y * s, a.z * s);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dDot(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dCross(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.y * b.z - a.z * b.y, a.z * b.x - a.x * b.z,
+                a.x * b.y - a.y * b.x);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dNorm(Vec3 v) {
+    return sqrtf(dDot(v, v));
+}
+
+__device__ val_t dRayTriangle(Vec3 orig, Vec3 dir, const Triangle& tri) {
+    Vec3 e1 = dSub(tri.b, tri.a), e2 = dSub(tri.c, tri.a);
+    Vec3 p = dCross(dir, e2);
+    val_t det = dDot(e1, p);
+    if (fabsf(det) < EPSILON) return 1.0e30f;
+    val_t inv = 1.0f / det;
+    Vec3 t = dSub(orig, tri.a);
+    val_t u = dDot(t, p) * inv;
+    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return 1.0e30f;
+    Vec3 q = dCross(t, e1);
+    val_t v = dDot(dir, q) * inv;
+    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return 1.0e30f;
+    return dDot(e2, q) * inv;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t dHash(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU;
+    x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bU;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dRandom(uint32_t seed) {
+    return (dHash(seed) >> 8) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
+}
+__device__ Vec3 dSample(const Triangle& tri, uint32_t seed) {
+    val_t u = dRandom(seed), v = dRandom(seed + 0x9e3779b9U);
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return dAdd(tri.a, dAdd(dMul(dSub(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                           dMul(dSub(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* tau, size_t n) {
+    size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t count = n * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    size_t i = pair / n, j = pair % n;
+    if (i == j) { tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    Vec3 delta = dSub(dMul(dAdd(dAdd(triangles[i].a, triangles[i].b),
+                               triangles[i].c), 1.0f / 3.0f),
+                      dMul(dAdd(dAdd(triangles[j].a, triangles[j].b),
+                               triangles[j].c), 1.0f / 3.0f));
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(dNorm(delta) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void formFactorKernel(const Triangle* triangles, val_t* kij, size_t n) {
+    size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t count = n * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    size_t i = pair / n, j = pair % n;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    if (dDot(a._normal, b._normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        Vec3 p = dSample(a, static_cast<uint32_t>(pair * NUM_RAYS + r * 2 + 1));
+        Vec3 q = dSample(b, static_cast<uint32_t>(pair * NUM_RAYS + r * 2 + 2));
+        Vec3 v = dSub(q, p);
+        val_t length = dNorm(v);
+        if (length <= EPSILON) continue;
+        Vec3 dir = dMul(v, 1.0f / length);
+        bool blocked = false;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+            if (k == i || k == j) continue;
+            val_t hit = dRayTriangle(p, dir, triangles[k]);
+            if (hit > EPSILON && hit < length - EPSILON) { blocked = true; break; }
+        }
+        if (blocked) continue;
+        val_t d2 = dDot(v, v);
+        val_t ci = fmaxf(0.0f, dDot(v, a._normal) / length);
+        val_t cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -dDot(v, b._normal) / length);
+        if (ci > 0.0f && cj > 0.0f) sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  const val_t* emission, val_t* reflected,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (t < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[i * n + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t source = reflected[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+    }
+    reflected[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* reflected, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += reflected[ (tt - t) * n + source] * reflected[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+template <typename T> T* deviceCopy(const std::vector<T>& input) {
+    T* result = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&result), input.size() * sizeof(T)), "cudaMalloc");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(result, input.data(), input.size() * sizeof(T), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "cudaMemcpy H2D");
+    return result;
+}
+
+void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing time delays (Tau) on CUDA...\n");
+    Triangle* dTriangles = deviceCopy(s.triangles);
+    int* dTau = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dTau), s.tau.size() * sizeof(int)), "cudaMalloc tau");
+    size_t count = s.numTriangles * s.numTriangles;
+    timeDelayKernel<<<(count + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dTriangles, dTau, s.numTriangles);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "timeDelayKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "timeDelayKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.tau.data(), dTau, s.tau.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy tau");
+    cudaFree(dTau); cudaFree(dTriangles);
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij) on CUDA...\n");
+    Triangle* dTriangles = deviceCopy(s.triangles);
+    val_t* dKij = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dKij), s.kij.size() * sizeof(val_t)), "cudaMalloc Kij");
+    size_t count = s.numTriangles * s.numTriangles;
+    formFactorKernel<<<(count + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dTriangles, dKij, s.numTriangles);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "formFactorKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "formFactorKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.kij.data(), dKij, s.kij.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy Kij");
+    cudaFree(dKij); cudaFree(dTriangles);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Running wave propagation simulation on CUDA...\n");
+    int* dTau = deviceCopy(s.tau); val_t* dKij = deviceCopy(s.kij);
+    val_t* dAreas = deviceCopy(s.areas); val_t* dRho = deviceCopy(s.rho);
+    val_t* dEmission = deviceCopy(s.radE); val_t* dReflected = deviceCopy(s.radB);
+    size_t blocks = (s.numTriangles + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < s.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        propagationKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dEmission, dReflected, s.numTriangles, t);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "propagationKernel");
+    }
+    cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "propagation sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.radB.data(), dReflected, s.radB.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy radiosity");
+    cudaFree(dTau); cudaFree(dKij); cudaFree(dAreas); cudaFree(dRho); cudaFree(dEmission); cudaFree(dReflected);
+}
+
+void computeDistances(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation on CUDA...\n");
+    val_t* dReflected = deviceCopy(s.radB); val_t* dDistances = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dDistances), s.distances.size() * sizeof(val_t)), "cudaMalloc distances");
+    size_t blocks = (s.numTriangles + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    distanceKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dReflected, dDistances, s.numTriangles, s.numTimesteps, s.sourceIndex);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "distanceKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "distanceKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.distances.data(), dDistances, s.distances.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy distances");
+    cudaFree(dReflected); cudaFree(dDistances);

 }
 
+#ifdef __CUDACC__
+
+// The numerical kernels use one independent CUDA thread per matrix element (or
+// receiver).  The timestep loop remains on the host because each timestep
+// depends on the preceding one; each launch is a global device barrier.
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+
+namespace {
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dAdd(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dSub(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dMul(Vec3 a, val_t s) {
+    return Vec3(a.x * s, a.y * s, a.z * s);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dDot(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dCross(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.y * b.z - a.z * b.y, a.z * b.x - a.x * b.z,
+                a.x * b.y - a.y * b.x);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dNorm(Vec3 v) {
+    return sqrtf(dDot(v, v));
+}
+
+__device__ val_t dRayTriangle(Vec3 orig, Vec3 dir, const Triangle& tri) {
+    Vec3 e1 = dSub(tri.b, tri.a), e2 = dSub(tri.c, tri.a);
+    Vec3 p = dCross(dir, e2);
+    val_t det = dDot(e1, p);
+    if (fabsf(det) < EPSILON) return 1.0e30f;
+    val_t inv = 1.0f / det;
+    Vec3 t = dSub(orig, tri.a);
+    val_t u = dDot(t, p) * inv;
+    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return 1.0e30f;
+    Vec3 q = dCross(t, e1);
+    val_t v = dDot(dir, q) * inv;
+    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return 1.0e30f;
+    return dDot(e2, q) * inv;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t dHash(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU;
+    x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bU;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dRandom(uint32_t seed) {
+    return (dHash(seed) >> 8) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
+}
+__device__ Vec3 dSample(const Triangle& tri, uint32_t seed) {
+    val_t u = dRandom(seed), v = dRandom(seed + 0x9e3779b9U);
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return dAdd(tri.a, dAdd(dMul(dSub(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                           dMul(dSub(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* tau, size_t n) {
+    size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t count = n * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    size_t i = pair / n, j = pair % n;
+    if (i == j) { tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    Vec3 delta = dSub(dMul(dAdd(dAdd(triangles[i].a, triangles[i].b),
+                               triangles[i].c), 1.0f / 3.0f),
+                      dMul(dAdd(dAdd(triangles[j].a, triangles[j].b),
+                               triangles[j].c), 1.0f / 3.0f));
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(dNorm(delta) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void formFactorKernel(const Triangle* triangles, val_t* kij, size_t n) {
+    size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t count = n * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    size_t i = pair / n, j = pair % n;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    if (dDot(a._normal, b._normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        Vec3 p = dSample(a, static_cast<uint32_t>(pair * NUM_RAYS + r * 2 + 1));
+        Vec3 q = dSample(b, static_cast<uint32_t>(pair * NUM_RAYS + r * 2 + 2));
+        Vec3 v = dSub(q, p);
+        val_t length = dNorm(v);
+        if (length <= EPSILON) continue;
+        Vec3 dir = dMul(v, 1.0f / length);
+        bool blocked = false;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+            if (k == i || k == j) continue;
+            val_t hit = dRayTriangle(p, dir, triangles[k]);
+            if (hit > EPSILON && hit < length - EPSILON) { blocked = true; break; }
+        }
+        if (blocked) continue;
+        val_t d2 = dDot(v, v);
+        val_t ci = fmaxf(0.0f, dDot(v, a._normal) / length);
+        val_t cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -dDot(v, b._normal) / length);
+        if (ci > 0.0f && cj > 0.0f) sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  const val_t* emission, val_t* reflected,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (t < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[i * n + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t source = reflected[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+    }
+    reflected[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* reflected, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += reflected[ (tt - t) * n + source] * reflected[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+template <typename T> T* deviceCopy(const std::vector<T>& input) {
+    T* result = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&result), input.size() * sizeof(T)), "cudaMalloc");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(result, input.data(), input.size() * sizeof(T), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "cudaMemcpy H2D");
+    return result;
+}
+
+void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing time delays (Tau) on CUDA...\n");
+    Triangle* dTriangles = deviceCopy(s.triangles);
+    int* dTau = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dTau), s.tau.size() * sizeof(int)), "cudaMalloc tau");
+    size_t count = s.numTriangles * s.numTriangles;
+    timeDelayKernel<<<(count + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dTriangles, dTau, s.numTriangles);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "timeDelayKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "timeDelayKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.tau.data(), dTau, s.tau.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy tau");
+    cudaFree(dTau); cudaFree(dTriangles);
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij) on CUDA...\n");
+    Triangle* dTriangles = deviceCopy(s.triangles);
+    val_t* dKij = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dKij), s.kij.size() * sizeof(val_t)), "cudaMalloc Kij");
+    size_t count = s.numTriangles * s.numTriangles;
+    formFactorKernel<<<(count + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dTriangles, dKij, s.numTriangles);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "formFactorKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "formFactorKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.kij.data(), dKij, s.kij.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy Kij");
+    cudaFree(dKij); cudaFree(dTriangles);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Running wave propagation simulation on CUDA...\n");
+    int* dTau = deviceCopy(s.tau); val_t* dKij = deviceCopy(s.kij);
+    val_t* dAreas = deviceCopy(s.areas); val_t* dRho = deviceCopy(s.rho);
+    val_t* dEmission = deviceCopy(s.radE); val_t* dReflected = deviceCopy(s.radB);
+    size_t blocks = (s.numTriangles + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < s.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        propagationKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dEmission, dReflected, s.numTriangles, t);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "propagationKernel");
+    }
+    cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "propagation sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.radB.data(), dReflected, s.radB.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy radiosity");
+    cudaFree(dTau); cudaFree(dKij); cudaFree(dAreas); cudaFree(dRho); cudaFree(dEmission); cudaFree(dReflected);
+}
+
+void computeDistances(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation on CUDA...\n");
+    val_t* dReflected = deviceCopy(s.radB); val_t* dDistances = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dDistances), s.distances.size() * sizeof(val_t)), "cudaMalloc distances");
+    size_t blocks = (s.numTriangles + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    distanceKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dReflected, dDistances, s.numTriangles, s.numTimesteps, s.sourceIndex);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "distanceKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "distanceKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.distances.data(), dDistances, s.distances.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy distances");
+    cudaFree(dReflected); cudaFree(dDistances);
+}
+
+} // namespace
+#else
+// This branch is retained only so the translation unit remains readable to
+// host-side tools; CMake always compiles roomsim.cpp as CUDA.
+void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& s) { computeTimeDelaysCPU(s); }
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& s) { computeFormFactorsCPU(s); }
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& s) { runSimulationCPU(s); }
+void computeDistances(SimulationState& s) { computeDistancesCPU(s); }
+#endif
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Validation
 // ============================================================================

+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dAdd(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dSub(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dMul(Vec3 a, val_t s) {
+    return Vec3(a.x * s, a.y * s, a.z * s);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dDot(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dCross(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.y * b.z - a.z * b.y, a.z * b.x - a.x * b.z,
+                a.x * b.y - a.y * b.x);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dNorm(Vec3 v) {
+    return sqrtf(dDot(v, v));
+}
+
+__device__ val_t dRayTriangle(Vec3 orig, Vec3 dir, const Triangle& tri) {
+    Vec3 e1 = dSub(tri.b, tri.a), e2 = dSub(tri.c, tri.a);
+    Vec3 p = dCross(dir, e2);
+    val_t det = dDot(e1, p);
+    if (fabsf(det) < EPSILON) return 1.0e30f;
+    val_t inv = 1.0f / det;
+    Vec3 t = dSub(orig, tri.a);
+    val_t u = dDot(t, p) * inv;
+    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return 1.0e30f;
+    Vec3 q = dCross(t, e1);
+    val_t v = dDot(dir, q) * inv;
+    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return 1.0e30f;
+    return dDot(e2, q) * inv;
+}

+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t dHash(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU;
+    x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bU;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dRandom(uint32_t seed) {
+    return (dHash(seed) >> 8) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
+}
+__device__ Vec3 dSample(const Triangle& tri, uint32_t seed) {
+    val_t u = dRandom(seed), v = dRandom(seed + 0x9e3779b9U);
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return dAdd(tri.a, dAdd(dMul(dSub(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                           dMul(dSub(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* tau, size_t n) {
+    size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t count = n * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    size_t i = pair / n, j = pair % n;
+    if (i == j) { tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    Vec3 delta = dSub(dMul(dAdd(dAdd(triangles[i].a, triangles[i].b),
+                               triangles[i].c), 1.0f / 3.0f),
+                      dMul(dAdd(dAdd(triangles[j].a, triangles[j].b),
+                               triangles[j].c), 1.0f / 3.0f));
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(dNorm(delta) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void formFactorKernel(const Triangle* triangles, val_t* kij, size_t n) {
+    size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t count = n * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    size_t i = pair / n, j = pair % n;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    if (dDot(a._normal, b._normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        Vec3 p = dSample(a, static_cast<uint32_t>(pair * NUM_RAYS + r * 2 + 1));
+        Vec3 q = dSample(b, static_cast<uint32_t>(pair * NUM_RAYS + r * 2 + 2));
+        Vec3 v = dSub(q, p);
+        val_t length = dNorm(v);
+        if (length <= EPSILON) continue;
+        Vec3 dir = dMul(v, 1.0f / length);
+        bool blocked = false;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+            if (k == i || k == j) continue;
+            val_t hit = dRayTriangle(p, dir, triangles[k]);
+            if (hit > EPSILON && hit < length - EPSILON) { blocked = true; break; }
+        }
+        if (blocked) continue;
+        val_t d2 = dDot(v, v);
+        val_t ci = fmaxf(0.0f, dDot(v, a._normal) / length);
+        val_t cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -dDot(v, b._normal) / length);
+        if (ci > 0.0f && cj > 0.0f) sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  const val_t* emission, val_t* reflected,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (t < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[i * n + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t source = reflected[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+    }
+    reflected[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* reflected, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += reflected[ (tt - t) * n + source] * reflected[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+template <typename T> T* deviceCopy(const std::vector<T>& input) {
+    T* result = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&result), input.size() * sizeof(T)), "cudaMalloc");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(result, input.data(), input.size() * sizeof(T), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "cudaMemcpy H2D");
+    return result;
+}
+
+void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing time delays (Tau) on CUDA...\n");
+    Triangle* dTriangles = deviceCopy(s.triangles);
+    int* dTau = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dTau), s.tau.size() * sizeof(int)), "cudaMalloc tau");
+    size_t count = s.numTriangles * s.numTriangles;
+    timeDelayKernel<<<(count + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dTriangles, dTau, s.numTriangles);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "timeDelayKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "timeDelayKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.tau.data(), dTau, s.tau.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy tau");
+    cudaFree(dTau); cudaFree(dTriangles);
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij) on CUDA...\n");
+    Triangle* dTriangles = deviceCopy(s.triangles);
+    val_t* dKij = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dKij), s.kij.size() * sizeof(val_t)), "cudaMalloc Kij");
+    size_t count = s.numTriangles * s.numTriangles;
+    formFactorKernel<<<(count + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dTriangles, dKij, s.numTriangles);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "formFactorKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "formFactorKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.kij.data(), dKij, s.kij.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy Kij");
+    cudaFree(dKij); cudaFree(dTriangles);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Running wave propagation simulation on CUDA...\n");
+    int* dTau = deviceCopy(s.tau); val_t* dKij = deviceCopy(s.kij);
+    val_t* dAreas = deviceCopy(s.areas); val_t* dRho = deviceCopy(s.rho);
+    val_t* dEmission = deviceCopy(s.radE); val_t* dReflected = deviceCopy(s.radB);
+    size_t blocks = (s.numTriangles + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < s.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        propagationKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dEmission, dReflected, s.numTriangles, t);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "propagationKernel");
+    }
+    cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "propagation sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.radB.data(), dReflected, s.radB.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy radiosity");
+    cudaFree(dTau); cudaFree(dKij); cudaFree(dAreas); cudaFree(dRho); cudaFree(dEmission); cudaFree(dReflected);
+}
+
+void computeDistances(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation on CUDA...\n");
+    val_t* dReflected = deviceCopy(s.radB); val_t* dDistances = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dDistances), s.distances.size() * sizeof(val_t)), "cudaMalloc distances");
+    size_t blocks = (s.numTriangles + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    distanceKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dReflected, dDistances, s.numTriangles, s.numTimesteps, s.sourceIndex);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "distanceKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "distanceKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.distances.data(), dDistances, s.distances.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy distances");
+    cudaFree(dReflected); cudaFree(dDistances);
+}
+
+} // namespace
+#else
+// This branch is retained only so the translation unit remains readable to
+// host-side tools; CMake always compiles roomsim.cpp as CUDA.
+void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& s) { computeTimeDelaysCPU(s); }
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& s) { computeFormFactorsCPU(s); }
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& s) { runSimulationCPU(s); }
+void computeDistances(SimulationState& s) { computeDistancesCPU(s); }
+#endif
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Validation
 // ============================================================================

                          show context between diff hunks up to the specified number of lines
    --output-indicator-new <char>
                          specify the character to indicate a new line instead of '+'
    --output-indicator-old <char>
                          specify the character to indicate an old line instead of '-'
    --output-indicator-context <char>
                          specify the character to indicate a context instead of ' '

Diff rename options
    -B, --break-rewrites[=<n>[/<m>]]
                          break complete rewrite changes into pairs of delete and create
    -M, --find-renames[=<n>]
                          detect renames
    -D, --irreversible-delete
                          omit the preimage for deletes
    -C, --find-copies[=<n>]
                          detect copies
    --[no-]find-copies-harder
                          use unmodified files as source to find copies
    --no-renames          disable rename detection
    --[no-]rename-empty   use empty blobs as rename source
    --[no-]follow         continue listing the history of a file beyond renames
    -l <n>                prevent rename/copy detection if the number of rename/copy targets exceeds given limit

Diff algorithm options
    --minimal             produce the smallest possible diff
    -w, --ignore-all-space
                          ignore whitespace when comparing lines
    -b, --ignore-space-change
                          ignore changes in amount of whitespace
    --ignore-space-at-eol ignore changes in whitespace at EOL
    --ignore-cr-at-eol    ignore carrier-return at the end of line
    --ignore-blank-lines  ignore changes whose lines are all blank
    -I, --[no-]ignore-matching-lines <regex>
                          ignore changes whose all lines match <regex>
    --[no-]indent-heuristic
                          heuristic to shift diff hunk boundaries for easy reading
    --patience            generate diff using the "patience diff" algorithm
    --histogram           generate diff using the "histogram diff" algorithm
    --diff-algorithm <algorithm>
                          choose a diff algorithm
    --anchored <text>     generate diff using the "anchored diff" algorithm
    --word-diff[=<mode>]  show word diff, using <mode> to delimit changed words
    --word-diff-regex <regex>
                          use <regex> to decide what a word is
    --color-words[=<regex>]
                          equivalent to --word-diff=color --word-diff-regex=<regex>
    --[no-]color-moved[=<mode>]
                          moved lines of code are colored differently
    --[no-]color-moved-ws <mode>
                          how white spaces are ignored in --color-moved

Other diff options
    --[no-]relative[=<prefix>]
                          when run from subdir, exclude changes outside and show relative paths
    -a, --[no-]text       treat all files as text
    -R                    swap two inputs, reverse the diff
    --[no-]exit-code      exit with 1 if there were differences, 0 otherwise
    --[no-]quiet          disable all output of the program
    --[no-]ext-diff       allow an external diff helper to be executed
    --[no-]textconv       run external text conversion filters when comparing binary files
    --ignore-submodules[=<when>]
                          ignore changes to submodules in the diff generation
    --submodule[=<format>]
                          specify how differences in submodules are shown
    --ita-invisible-in-index
                          hide 'git add -N' entries from the index
    --ita-visible-in-index
                          treat 'git add -N' entries as real in the index
    -S <string>           look for differences that change the number of occurrences of the specified string
    -G <regex>            look for differences that change the number of occurrences of the specified regex
    --pickaxe-all         show all changes in the changeset with -S or -G
    --pickaxe-regex       treat <string> in -S as extended POSIX regular expression
    -O <file>             control the order in which files appear in the output
    --rotate-to <path>    show the change in the specified path first
    --skip-to <path>      skip the output to the specified path
    --find-object <object-id>
                          look for differences that change the number of occurrences of the specified object
    --diff-filter [(A|C|D|M|R|T|U|X|B)...[*]]
                          select files by diff type
    --output <file>       output to a specific file

 }
 
+#ifdef __CUDACC__
+
+// The numerical kernels use one independent CUDA thread per matrix element (or
+// receiver).  The timestep loop remains on the host because each timestep
+// depends on the preceding one; each launch is a global device barrier.
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+
+namespace {
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dAdd(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dSub(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dMul(Vec3 a, val_t s) {
+    return Vec3(a.x * s, a.y * s, a.z * s);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dDot(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dCross(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.y * b.z - a.z * b.y, a.z * b.x - a.x * b.z,
+                a.x * b.y - a.y * b.x);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dNorm(Vec3 v) {
+    return sqrtf(dDot(v, v));
+}
+
+__device__ val_t dRayTriangle(Vec3 orig, Vec3 dir, const Triangle& tri) {
+    Vec3 e1 = dSub(tri.b, tri.a), e2 = dSub(tri.c, tri.a);
+    Vec3 p = dCross(dir, e2);
+    val_t det = dDot(e1, p);
+    if (fabsf(det) < EPSILON) return 1.0e30f;
+    val_t inv = 1.0f / det;
+    Vec3 t = dSub(orig, tri.a);
+    val_t u = dDot(t, p) * inv;
+    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return 1.0e30f;
+    Vec3 q = dCross(t, e1);
+    val_t v = dDot(dir, q) * inv;
+    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return 1.0e30f;
+    return dDot(e2, q) * inv;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t dHash(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU;
+    x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bU;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dRandom(uint32_t seed) {
+    return (dHash(seed) >> 8) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
+}
+__device__ Vec3 dSample(const Triangle& tri, uint32_t seed) {
+    val_t u = dRandom(seed), v = dRandom(seed + 0x9e3779b9U);
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return dAdd(tri.a, dAdd(dMul(dSub(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                           dMul(dSub(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* tau, size_t n) {
+    size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t count = n * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    size_t i = pair / n, j = pair % n;
+    if (i == j) { tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    Vec3 delta = dSub(dMul(dAdd(dAdd(triangles[i].a, triangles[i].b),
+                               triangles[i].c), 1.0f / 3.0f),
+                      dMul(dAdd(dAdd(triangles[j].a, triangles[j].b),
+                               triangles[j].c), 1.0f / 3.0f));
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(dNorm(delta) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void formFactorKernel(const Triangle* triangles, val_t* kij, size_t n) {
+    size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t count = n * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    size_t i = pair / n, j = pair % n;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    if (dDot(a._normal, b._normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        Vec3 p = dSample(a, static_cast<uint32_t>(pair * NUM_RAYS + r * 2 + 1));
+        Vec3 q = dSample(b, static_cast<uint32_t>(pair * NUM_RAYS + r * 2 + 2));
+        Vec3 v = dSub(q, p);
+        val_t length = dNorm(v);
+        if (length <= EPSILON) continue;
+        Vec3 dir = dMul(v, 1.0f / length);
+        bool blocked = false;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+            if (k == i || k == j) continue;
+            val_t hit = dRayTriangle(p, dir, triangles[k]);
+            if (hit > EPSILON && hit < length - EPSILON) { blocked = true; break; }
+        }
+        if (blocked) continue;
+        val_t d2 = dDot(v, v);
+        val_t ci = fmaxf(0.0f, dDot(v, a._normal) / length);
+        val_t cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -dDot(v, b._normal) / length);
+        if (ci > 0.0f && cj > 0.0f) sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  const val_t* emission, val_t* reflected,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (t < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[i * n + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t source = reflected[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+    }
+    reflected[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* reflected, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += reflected[ (tt - t) * n + source] * reflected[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+template <typename T> T* deviceCopy(const std::vector<T>& input) {
+    T* result = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&result), input.size() * sizeof(T)), "cudaMalloc");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(result, input.data(), input.size() * sizeof(T), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "cudaMemcpy H2D");
+    return result;
+}
+
+void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing time delays (Tau) on CUDA...\n");
+    Triangle* dTriangles = deviceCopy(s.triangles);
+    int* dTau = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dTau), s.tau.size() * sizeof(int)), "cudaMalloc tau");
+    size_t count = s.numTriangles * s.numTriangles;
+    timeDelayKernel<<<(count + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dTriangles, dTau, s.numTriangles);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "timeDelayKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "timeDelayKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.tau.data(), dTau, s.tau.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy tau");
+    cudaFree(dTau); cudaFree(dTriangles);
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij) on CUDA...\n");
+    Triangle* dTriangles = deviceCopy(s.triangles);
+    val_t* dKij = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dKij), s.kij.size() * sizeof(val_t)), "cudaMalloc Kij");
+    size_t count = s.numTriangles * s.numTriangles;
+    formFactorKernel<<<(count + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dTriangles, dKij, s.numTriangles);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "formFactorKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "formFactorKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.kij.data(), dKij, s.kij.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy Kij");
+    cudaFree(dKij); cudaFree(dTriangles);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Running wave propagation simulation on CUDA...\n");
+    int* dTau = deviceCopy(s.tau); val_t* dKij = deviceCopy(s.kij);
+    val_t* dAreas = deviceCopy(s.areas); val_t* dRho = deviceCopy(s.rho);
+    val_t* dEmission = deviceCopy(s.radE); val_t* dReflected = deviceCopy(s.radB);
+    size_t blocks = (s.numTriangles + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < s.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        propagationKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dEmission, dReflected, s.numTriangles, t);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "propagationKernel");
+    }
+    cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "propagation sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.radB.data(), dReflected, s.radB.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy radiosity");
+    cudaFree(dTau); cudaFree(dKij); cudaFree(dAreas); cudaFree(dRho); cudaFree(dEmission); cudaFree(dReflected);
+}
+
+void computeDistances(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation on CUDA...\n");
+    val_t* dReflected = deviceCopy(s.radB); val_t* dDistances = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dDistances), s.distances.size() * sizeof(val_t)), "cudaMalloc distances");
+    size_t blocks = (s.numTriangles + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    distanceKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dReflected, dDistances, s.numTriangles, s.numTimesteps, s.sourceIndex);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "distanceKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "distanceKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.distances.data(), dDistances, s.distances.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy distances");
+    cudaFree(dReflected); cudaFree(dDistances);
+}
+
+} // namespace
+#else
+// This branch is retained only so the translation unit remains readable to
+// host-side tools; CMake always compiles roomsim.cpp as CUDA.
+void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& s) { computeTimeDelaysCPU(s); }
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& s) { computeFormFactorsCPU(s); }
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& s) { runSimulationCPU(s); }
+void computeDistances(SimulationState& s) { computeDistancesCPU(s); }
+#endif
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Validation
 // ============================================================================

+#ifdef __CUDACC__
+
+// The numerical kernels use one independent CUDA thread per matrix element (or
+// receiver).  The timestep loop remains on the host because each timestep
+// depends on the preceding one; each launch is a global device barrier.
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+
+namespace {
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dAdd(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dSub(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dMul(Vec3 a, val_t s) {
+    return Vec3(a.x * s, a.y * s, a.z * s);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dDot(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dCross(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.y * b.z - a.z * b.y, a.z * b.x - a.x * b.z,
+                a.x * b.y - a.y * b.x);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dNorm(Vec3 v) {
+    return sqrtf(dDot(v, v));
+}
+
+__device__ val_t dRayTriangle(Vec3 orig, Vec3 dir, const Triangle& tri) {
+    Vec3 e1 = dSub(tri.b, tri.a), e2 = dSub(tri.c, tri.a);
+    Vec3 p = dCross(dir, e2);
+    val_t det = dDot(e1, p);
+    if (fabsf(det) < EPSILON) return 1.0e30f;
+    val_t inv = 1.0f / det;
+    Vec3 t = dSub(orig, tri.a);
+    val_t u = dDot(t, p) * inv;
+    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return 1.0e30f;
+    Vec3 q = dCross(t, e1);
+    val_t v = dDot(dir, q) * inv;
+    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return 1.0e30f;
+    return dDot(e2, q) * inv;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t dHash(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU;
+    x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bU;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dRandom(uint32_t seed) {
+    return (dHash(seed) >> 8) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
+}
+__device__ Vec3 dSample(const Triangle& tri, uint32_t seed) {
+    val_t u = dRandom(seed), v = dRandom(seed + 0x9e3779b9U);
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return dAdd(tri.a, dAdd(dMul(dSub(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                           dMul(dSub(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* tau, size_t n) {
+    size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t count = n * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    size_t i = pair / n, j = pair % n;
+    if (i == j) { tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    Vec3 delta = dSub(dMul(dAdd(dAdd(triangles[i].a, triangles[i].b),
+                               triangles[i].c), 1.0f / 3.0f),
+                      dMul(dAdd(dAdd(triangles[j].a, triangles[j].b),
+                               triangles[j].c), 1.0f / 3.0f));
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(dNorm(delta) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void formFactorKernel(const Triangle* triangles, val_t* kij, size_t n) {
+    size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t count = n * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    size_t i = pair / n, j = pair % n;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    if (dDot(a._normal, b._normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        Vec3 p = dSample(a, static_cast<uint32_t>(pair * NUM_RAYS + r * 2 + 1));
+        Vec3 q = dSample(b, static_cast<uint32_t>(pair * NUM_RAYS + r * 2 + 2));
+        Vec3 v = dSub(q, p);
+        val_t length = dNorm(v);
+        if (length <= EPSILON) continue;
+        Vec3 dir = dMul(v, 1.0f / length);
+        bool blocked = false;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+            if (k == i || k == j) continue;
+            val_t hit = dRayTriangle(p, dir, triangles[k]);
+            if (hit > EPSILON && hit < length - EPSILON) { blocked = true; break; }
+        }
+        if (blocked) continue;
+        val_t d2 = dDot(v, v);
+        val_t ci = fmaxf(0.0f, dDot(v, a._normal) / length);
+        val_t cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -dDot(v, b._normal) / length);
+        if (ci > 0.0f && cj > 0.0f) sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  const val_t* emission, val_t* reflected,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[i * n + j];
+        if (t < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[i * n + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t source = reflected[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+    }
+    reflected[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* reflected, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += reflected[ (tt - t) * n + source] * reflected[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+template <typename T> T* deviceCopy(const std::vector<T>& input) {
+    T* result = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&result), input.size() * sizeof(T)), "cudaMalloc");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(result, input.data(), input.size() * sizeof(T), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "cudaMemcpy H2D");
+    return result;
+}
+
+void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing time delays (Tau) on CUDA...\n");
+    Triangle* dTriangles = deviceCopy(s.triangles);
+    int* dTau = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dTau), s.tau.size() * sizeof(int)), "cudaMalloc tau");
+    size_t count = s.numTriangles * s.numTriangles;
+    timeDelayKernel<<<(count + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dTriangles, dTau, s.numTriangles);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "timeDelayKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "timeDelayKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.tau.data(), dTau, s.tau.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy tau");
+    cudaFree(dTau); cudaFree(dTriangles);
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij) on CUDA...\n");
+    Triangle* dTriangles = deviceCopy(s.triangles);
+    val_t* dKij = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dKij), s.kij.size() * sizeof(val_t)), "cudaMalloc Kij");
+    size_t count = s.numTriangles * s.numTriangles;
+    formFactorKernel<<<(count + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dTriangles, dKij, s.numTriangles);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "formFactorKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "formFactorKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.kij.data(), dKij, s.kij.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy Kij");
+    cudaFree(dKij); cudaFree(dTriangles);
+}
+
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Running wave propagation simulation on CUDA...\n");
+    int* dTau = deviceCopy(s.tau); val_t* dKij = deviceCopy(s.kij);
+    val_t* dAreas = deviceCopy(s.areas); val_t* dRho = deviceCopy(s.rho);
+    val_t* dEmission = deviceCopy(s.radE); val_t* dReflected = deviceCopy(s.radB);
+    size_t blocks = (s.numTriangles + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < s.numTimesteps; ++t) {
+        propagationKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dEmission, dReflected, s.numTriangles, t);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "propagationKernel");
+    }
+    cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "propagation sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.radB.data(), dReflected, s.radB.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy radiosity");
+    cudaFree(dTau); cudaFree(dKij); cudaFree(dAreas); cudaFree(dRho); cudaFree(dEmission); cudaFree(dReflected);
+}
+
+void computeDistances(SimulationState& s) {
+    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation on CUDA...\n");
+    val_t* dReflected = deviceCopy(s.radB); val_t* dDistances = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dDistances), s.distances.size() * sizeof(val_t)), "cudaMalloc distances");
+    size_t blocks = (s.numTriangles + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
+    distanceKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(dReflected, dDistances, s.numTriangles, s.numTimesteps, s.sourceIndex);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "distanceKernel"); cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "distanceKernel sync");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(s.distances.data(), dDistances, s.distances.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy distances");
+    cudaFree(dReflected); cudaFree(dDistances);
+}
+
+} // namespace
+#else
+// This branch is retained only so the translation unit remains readable to
+// host-side tools; CMake always compiles roomsim.cpp as CUDA.
+void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& s) { computeTimeDelaysCPU(s); }
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& s) { computeFormFactorsCPU(s); }
+void runSimulation(SimulationState& s) { runSimulationCPU(s); }
+void computeDistances(SimulationState& s) { computeDistancesCPU(s); }
+#endif
+

+#ifdef __CUDACC__
+
+// The numerical kernels use one independent CUDA thread per matrix element (or
+// receiver).  The timestep loop remains on the host because each timestep
+// depends on the preceding one; each launch is a global device barrier.
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+
+namespace {
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dAdd(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dSub(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dMul(Vec3 a, val_t s) {
+    return Vec3(a.x * s, a.y * s, a.z * s);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dDot(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 dCross(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) {
+    return Vec3(a.y * b.z - a.z * b.y, a.z * b.x - a.x * b.z,
+                a.x * b.y - a.y * b.x);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dNorm(Vec3 v) {
+    return sqrtf(dDot(v, v));
+}
+
+__device__ val_t dRayTriangle(Vec3 orig, Vec3 dir, const Triangle& tri) {
+    Vec3 e1 = dSub(tri.b, tri.a), e2 = dSub(tri.c, tri.a);
+    Vec3 p = dCross(dir, e2);
+    val_t det = dDot(e1, p);
+    if (fabsf(det) < EPSILON) return 1.0e30f;
+    val_t inv = 1.0f / det;
+    Vec3 t = dSub(orig, tri.a);
+    val_t u = dDot(t, p) * inv;
+    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return 1.0e30f;
+    Vec3 q = dCross(t, e1);
+    val_t v = dDot(dir, q) * inv;
+    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return 1.0e30f;
+    return dDot(e2, q) * inv;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t dHash(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU;
+    x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bU;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t dRandom(uint32_t seed) {
+    return (dHash(seed) >> 8) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
+}
+__device__ Vec3 dSample(const Triangle& tri, uint32_t seed) {
+    val_t u = dRandom(seed), v = dRandom(seed + 0x9e3779b9U);
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return dAdd(tri.a, dAdd(dMul(dSub(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                           dMul(dSub(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
